Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the math_analysis structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 794. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 416 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to math_analysis theory, we decompose the problem into 794 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 416. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)
